tools/mass_budget.py 30 satır
"""Robot govdesinin kutle butcesi: kutle merkezi yuksekligi ve ataleti (teker ekseni z=0).
Parca agirliklari tahmindir (OLC): parca gelince terazide tartip guncelleyin."""
# (ad, kutle kg, z m (eksenden yukseklik), x m (one +), boyut z m (atalet icin))
# Konumlar 3B modelden (cad/robot_cad.py YER ve kat yukseklikleri). Pil kat 2'nin ustunde, ana kart kat 2'nin altinda asili.
PARTS = [
    ("2x JGB37-520 motor + tutucu", 0.380, -0.006, 0.0, 0.037),
    ("Kat 1 baski 150x110x4",       0.060, 0.024, 0.0, 0.004),
    ("2x BTS7960",                  0.130, 0.0485, -0.021, 0.035),
    ("LM2596 + sigorta + 2 WAGO",   0.060, 0.033, 0.03, 0.020),
    ("MPU6050 (kat 1 ustu, ortada)", 0.005, 0.0285, 0.0, 0.003),
    ("Kat 2 baski",                 0.060, 0.108, 0.0, 0.004),
    ("3x18650 + yuva + BMS",        0.190, 0.1205, -0.017, 0.022),
    ("Ana kart + ESP32",            0.045, 0.087, -0.028, 0.015),
    ("IR gozleri + V-yuva + pimler", 0.035, 0.070, 0.055, 0.080),
    ("Kat 3 baski",                 0.060, 0.192, 0.0, 0.004),
    ("Acil stop",                   0.070, 0.195, 0.028, 0.060),
    ("Kutu yuvasi",                 0.030, 0.205, 0.016, 0.025),
    ("16x distans + vida + kablo",  0.120, 0.106, 0.0, 0.160),
]
BOX = ("Ilac kutusu", 0.060, 0.2065)  # cep merkezi x=+16 mm, y=+27 mm (cad/robot_cad.py)
m = sum(p[1] for p in PARTS)
l = sum(p[1] * p[2] for p in PARTS) / m
I = sum(p[1] * ((p[2] - l) ** 2 + (p[4] ** 2 + 0.11 ** 2) / 12) for p in PARTS)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    for p in PARTS:
        print(f"{p[0]:32s} {p[1]*1000:5.0f} g  z={p[2]*1000:4.0f} mm")
    print(f"govde (kutusuz): {m:.3f} kg, KM eksenin {l*1000:.0f} mm ustunde, I={I:.4f} kg m^2")
    mt = m + BOX[1]; lt = (m * l + BOX[1] * BOX[2]) / mt
    print(f"kutulu: {mt:.3f} kg, KM {lt*1000:.0f} mm")
tools/lqr_design.py 173 satır
"""Denge robotu modeli ve LQR kazanc tasarimi.

Calistir:  python tools/lqr_design.py
Uretir:    common/cdtp_core/src/gains.h   (firmware + simulasyon kazanclari)
           sim/plant_nominal.h            (simulasyonun nominal fiziksel modeli)

Parca gelince "OLC" yazan degerleri tartip olcup bu dosyada guncelleyin, yeniden calistirin.
"""
import math
import pathlib

import numpy as np
from scipy.linalg import expm, solve_discrete_are

import mass_budget

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

# ---- Fiziksel model (OLC) ----
P = dict(
    # govde degerleri tools/mass_budget.py'deki parca listesinden (kutu haric; kutuyu simulasyon ekler)
    m_body=round(mass_budget.m, 3),   # kg, tekerler haric her sey (motor govdeleri dahil)
    l_com=round(mass_budget.l, 4),    # m, teker ekseninden govde kutle merkezine
    I_body=round(mass_budget.I, 5),   # kg m^2, kutle merkezi etrafinda yunuslama ataleti
    m_wheel=0.070,      # kg, tek teker (60 g) + alüminyum adaptör
    r=0.035,            # m (70 mm teker)
    J_rotor=5.0e-7,     # kg m^2, motor rotoru (disli oncesi)
    gear=19.0,          # JGB37-520 530 RPM ~ 1:19
    Ke=0.216,           # V s/rad (cikis mili) = Kt (N m/A)
    R=4.5,              # ohm, sargi + surucu
    b_visc=0.002,       # N m s/rad, disli viskoz surtunme (motor basina)
    tau_coulomb=0.02,   # N m, disli kuru surtunme (motor basina)
    track=0.19,         # m, teker merkezleri arasi (sasi cizimi; OLC)
    I_yaw=0.012,        # kg m^2
    vbat=11.1,
)

DT = 1.0 / 200.0


def linear_model(p):
    """Durum s = [x, v, theta, omega], giris u = her motora ayni gerilim (V)."""
    r, mb, l = p["r"], p["m_body"], p["l_com"]
    Iw = 2 * 0.5 * p["m_wheel"] * r**2
    Jm = 2 * p["J_rotor"] * p["gear"] ** 2
    a = 2 * p["m_wheel"] * r**2 + Iw + mb * r**2 + Jm
    b = mb * r * l - Jm
    c = mb * l**2 + p["I_body"] + Jm
    M = np.array([[a, b], [b, c]])
    Minv = np.linalg.inv(M)
    k = 2 * p["Ke"] / p["R"]                      # iki motor, tork/gerilim
    d = 2 * (p["Ke"] ** 2 / p["R"] + p["b_visc"])  # iki motor, tork/(bagil hiz)
    g = 9.81
    # q = [phi, theta]; tork tau = k u - d (phi_dot - theta_dot), tekerlege +tau, govdeye -tau
    # M qdd = [tau, -tau + mb g l theta]
    # phi = x / r
    A = np.zeros((4, 4))
    B = np.zeros((4, 1))
    # qdd = Minv @ (F_u * u + F_phid * phid + F_thd * thd + F_th * th)
    F_u = np.array([k, -k])
    F_phid = np.array([-d, d])
    F_thd = np.array([d, -d])
    F_th = np.array([0.0, mb * g * l])
    acc_u = Minv @ F_u
    acc_phid = Minv @ F_phid
    acc_thd = Minv @ F_thd
    acc_th = Minv @ F_th
    # x' = v ; v' = r phidd ; phid = v / r
    A[0, 1] = 1
    A[1, 1] = r * acc_phid[0] / r
    A[1, 2] = r * acc_th[0]
    A[1, 3] = r * acc_thd[0]
    B[1, 0] = r * acc_u[0]
    A[2, 3] = 1
    A[3, 1] = acc_phid[1] / r
    A[3, 2] = acc_th[1]
    A[3, 3] = acc_thd[1]
    B[3, 0] = acc_u[1]
    return A, B


def discretize(A, B, dt):
    n, m = A.shape[0], B.shape[1]
    big = np.zeros((n + m, n + m))
    big[:n, :n] = A
    big[:n, n:] = B
    e = expm(big * dt)
    return e[:n, :n], e[:n, n:]


def dlqr(Ad, Bd, Q, R):
    X = solve_discrete_are(Ad, Bd, Q, R)
    return np.linalg.solve(Bd.T @ X @ Bd + R, Bd.T @ X @ Ad)


# Agirliklar: izin verilen en buyuk hata (Bryson kurali, agirlik = 1/hata^2)
W = dict(x=0.06, v=0.08, th_deg=4.0, om_dps=60.0, u_volt=5.0)  # sim ile secildi (sim/sim_main.cpp SAG testi)


def design(p, w=None):
    w = w or W
    A, B = linear_model(p)
    Ad, Bd = discretize(A, B, DT)
    Q = np.diag([1 / w["x"] ** 2, 1 / w["v"] ** 2, 1 / math.radians(w["th_deg"]) ** 2, 1 / math.radians(w["om_dps"]) ** 2])
    R = np.array([[1 / w["u_volt"] ** 2]])
    K = dlqr(Ad, Bd, Q, R)
    cl = np.linalg.eigvals(Ad - Bd @ K)
    return A, B, K.flatten(), cl


def yaw_gains(p):
    # yaw: I psi_dd = (track/2r) * 2*(Ke/R) * u_d - damping ; PD ile ~6 rad/s bant
    r, tr = p["r"], p["track"]
    kyaw = (tr / (2 * r)) * 2 * p["Ke"] / p["R"]
    wn, zeta = 8.0, 0.9
    kp = p["I_yaw"] * wn**2 / kyaw
    kd = p["I_yaw"] * 2 * zeta * wn / kyaw
    return kp, kd


def main():
    import sys
    for arg in sys.argv[1:]:  # ornek: x=0.05 v=0.1
        k, v = arg.split("=")
        W[k] = float(v)
    A, B, K, cl = design(P)
    print("agirliklar:", W)
    kp_y, kd_y = yaw_gains(P)
    print("A =\n", np.round(A, 3))
    print("B =", np.round(B.flatten(), 3))
    print("acik dongu kutuplar:", np.round(np.linalg.eigvals(A), 2))
    print("K [x, v, theta, omega] =", np.round(K, 3))
    print("kapali dongu |z|:", np.round(np.abs(cl), 4))
    print(f"yaw Kp={kp_y:.3f} V/rad  Kd={kd_y:.3f} V/(rad/s)")

    gains = f"""// OTOMATIK URETILDI: tools/lqr_design.py. Elle degistirmeyin; modeli guncelleyip yeniden calistirin.
// Durum: [x (m), v (m/s), theta (rad), omega (rad/s)], cikis: her motora gerilim (V).
// u = -K (s - s_ref)
#pragma once
namespace cdtp {{
namespace gains {{
constexpr float K_X = {K[0]:.5f}f;
constexpr float K_V = {K[1]:.5f}f;
constexpr float K_TH = {K[2]:.5f}f;
constexpr float K_OM = {K[3]:.5f}f;
constexpr float YAW_KP = {kp_y:.5f}f;
constexpr float YAW_KD = {kd_y:.5f}f;
// a_ref -> egim on-besleme (rad / (m/s^2))
constexpr float TH_PER_ACC = {th_per_acc(P):.5f}f;
}}  // namespace gains
}}  // namespace cdtp
"""
    (ROOT / "common/cdtp_core/src/gains.h").write_text(gains, encoding="utf-8")

    plant = "// OTOMATIK URETILDI: tools/lqr_design.py\n#pragma once\nstruct PlantNominal {\n"
    for k, v in P.items():
        plant += f"  static constexpr double {k} = {v!r};\n"
    plant += "};\n"
    (ROOT / "sim/plant_nominal.h").write_text(plant, encoding="utf-8")
    print("yazildi: gains.h, plant_nominal.h")


def th_per_acc(p):
    # sabit ivme a icin dengedeki egim: sin(th) = a (mb l + J/r) / (mb g l)
    r, mb, l = p["r"], p["m_body"], p["l_com"]
    Iw = 2 * 0.5 * p["m_wheel"] * r**2
    J = 2 * p["m_wheel"] * r**2 + Iw + mb * r**2
    return (mb * l + J / r) / (mb * 9.81 * l)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
tools/check_consistency.py 149 satır
"""Cizilen/yazilan belgeler ile kodun tutarlilik denetimi.

Karsilastirir:
  1. firmware/robot/src/pins.h  <->  takim sitesinin pin semasi (tools/ref/site_wiring.js)
  2. pins.h                     <->  docs/robot_src.html pin tablosu ve ana kart header siralari
  3. cdtp_config.h / gains.h    <->  belgelerde yazan sayilar
  4. links.h BLE UUID'leri      <->  docs/BLE_PROTOKOL.md
  5. bolucu oranlari            <->  pins.h DIVIDER ve belge
Cikti: docs/tutarlilik.md (+ ekrana ozet). Hata varsa cikis kodu 1.
"""
import pathlib
import re
import sys

sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
rd = lambda p: (ROOT / p).read_text(encoding="utf-8")

rows = []  # (konu, beklenen, bulunan, durum)


def check(topic, expected, found, ok=None):
    if ok is None:
        ok = str(expected) == str(found)
    rows.append((topic, str(expected), str(found), "uygun" if ok else "UYUMSUZ"))


pins_h = rd("firmware/robot/src/pins.h")
pins = {m[0]: int(m[1]) for m in re.findall(r"constexpr int (PIN_\w+) = (\d+);", pins_h)}

# ---- 1. site pin semasi ----
site = rd("tools/ref/site_wiring.js")
robot_block = site[site.index("export const ROBOT_PINS"):site.index("export const GLOVE_PINS")]
site_map = {}
for name, gpio in re.findall(r"\['([^']+)', 'GPIO(\d+)", robot_block):
    site_map.setdefault(name, []).append(int(gpio))
expect_site = {
    "SDA": ("PIN_SDA",), "SCL": ("PIN_SCL",), "INT": ("PIN_IMU_INT",),

}

    got = site_map.get(sig, [])
    want = [pins[n] for n in names]

tbl = {}
for pinsTxt, task in re.findall(r'<td class="num">([\d ,/]+)</td><td>([^<]+)</td>', sig_sec):

    "MPU6050 INT": [pins["PIN_IMU_INT"]],
    "I2C SDA / SCL": [pins["PIN_SDA"], pins["PIN_SCL"]],
    "Sol / sağ RPWM, LPWM": [pins["PIN_L_RPWM"], pins["PIN_L_LPWM"], pins["PIN_R_RPWM"], pins["PIN_R_LPWM"]],
    "EN (iki kart)": [pins["PIN_MOTOR_EN"]],
    "Enkoder A, B": [pins["PIN_ENC_L_A"], pins["PIN_ENC_L_B"], pins["PIN_ENC_R_A"], pins["PIN_ENC_R_B"]],
    "IR göz sol / sağ": [pins["PIN_IR_L"], pins["PIN_IR_R"]],
    "Pil / şarj pimi gerilimi": [pins["PIN_VBAT"], pins["PIN_VCHG"]],
    "Acil stop sonrası motor hattı": [pins["PIN_MOTOR_SENSE"]],
    "Karttaki LED": [pins["PIN_LED"]],
}
for task, want in doc_expect.items():
    check(f"Belge pin tablosu: {task}", want, tbl.get(task))

hdr_sec = doc[doc.index('id="anakart"'):doc.index('id="kablo"')]
headers = dict(re.findall(r'<td class="num">(J\d+)</td><td>([^<]+)</td>', hdr_sec))
hdr_expect = {
    "J2": f"3V3, GND, G{pins['PIN_SCL']}, G{pins['PIN_SDA']}, G{pins['PIN_IMU_INT']}",
    "J3": f"G{pins['PIN_L_RPWM']}, G{pins['PIN_L_LPWM']}, G{pins['PIN_MOTOR_EN']}, 3V3, GND",
    "J8": f"G{pins['PIN_R_RPWM']}, G{pins['PIN_R_LPWM']}, G{pins['PIN_MOTOR_EN']}, 3V3, GND",
    "J4": f"3V3, GND, G{pins['PIN_ENC_L_A']}, G{pins['PIN_ENC_L_B']}",
    "J5": f"3V3, GND, G{pins['PIN_ENC_R_A']}, G{pins['PIN_ENC_R_B']}",
}

    check(f"Ana kart {j} pin sirasi", want, headers.get(j, "").strip())

# ---- 3. sayilar ----

tools/plot_sim.py 94 satır
"""Simulasyon izlerini cizer: sim/results/*.csv -> docs/img/*.png"""
import csv
import pathlib

import matplotlib

matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RES = ROOT / "sim/results"
OUT = ROOT / "docs/img"
OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def load(name):
    with open(RES / name, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    return {k: [float(r[k]) for r in rows] for k in rows[0]}


def style(ax):
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    ax.spines[["top", "right"]].set_visible(False)


def motion(fname, title, out):
    d = load(fname)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(3, 1, figsize=(8, 6.4), sharex=True)
    ax[0].plot(d["t"], d["x"], color="#2a6fdb")
    ax[0].set_ylabel("yol x (m)")
    ax[1].plot(d["t"], d["theta_deg"], color="#d9822b")
    ax[1].set_ylabel("egim (deg)")
    ax[2].plot(d["t"], d["dutyL"], color="#2bb3a3", lw=0.8, label="sol")
    ax[2].plot(d["t"], [-v for v in d["dutyR"]], color="#9b7cf0", lw=0.8, label="sag (ayna)")
    ax[2].set_ylabel("PWM orani")
    ax[2].set_xlabel("zaman (s)")
    ax[2].legend(frameon=False, loc="upper right")
    for a in ax:
        style(a)
    fig.suptitle(title)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(OUT / out, dpi=110)
    plt.close(fig)


def recovery():
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 3.2))
    for fn, lab, c in (("c1_toparlanma_arti.csv", "+10 deg", "#d9822b"), ("c1_toparlanma_eksi.csv", "-10 deg", "#2a6fdb")):
        d = load(fn)
        ax.plot(d["t"], d["theta_deg"], label=lab, color=c)
    ax.axhspan(-3, 3, color="#2bb3a3", alpha=0.12, label="+-3 deg")
    ax.set_xlabel("zaman (s)")
    ax.set_ylabel("egim (deg)")
    ax.set_xlim(0.8, 6)
    ax.legend(frameon=False)
    style(ax)
    ax.set_title("C1.4: +-10 deg'den birakilinca toparlanma")
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(OUT / "c1_toparlanma.png", dpi=110)
    plt.close(fig)


def dock():
    import math

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6, 5))
    for fn, c in (("c3_yanasma.csv", "#2a6fdb"), ("c5_butunlesik.csv", "#d9822b")):
        d = load(fn)
        ax.plot(d["X"], d["Y"], color=c, label="C3 yanasma" if "c3" in fn else "C5 butunlesik gorev")
        ax.plot(d["X"][0], d["Y"][0], "o", color=c)
    ax.add_patch(plt.Circle((0, 0), 0.02, color="#333"))
    th = [math.radians(a) for a in range(-45, 46)]
    ax.plot([0.3 * math.cos(a) for a in th], [0.3 * math.sin(a) for a in th], "--", color="#999", lw=0.8)
    ax.plot([0, 0.3 * math.cos(th[0])], [0, 0.3 * math.sin(th[0])], "--", color="#999", lw=0.8)
    ax.plot([0, 0.3 * math.cos(th[-1])], [0, 0.3 * math.sin(th[-1])], "--", color="#999", lw=0.8, label="30 cm / 90 deg bolge")
    ax.set_aspect("equal")
    ax.set_xlabel("X (m)")
    ax.set_ylabel("Y (m)")
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=8, loc="lower left")
    style(ax)
    ax.set_title("Ustten gorunus: istasyon diregi (0,0)")
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(OUT / "yanasma_yolu.png", dpi=110)
    plt.close(fig)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    motion("c1_ileri.csv", "C1.2: iki 'ileri' komutu = 1 m, sonra 3 s dur", "c1_ileri.png")
    motion("c2_eldiven.csv", "C2: ileri, geri, sola, saga, dur", "c2_eldiven.png")
    recovery()
    dock()
    print("cizildi:", sorted(p.name for p in OUT.glob("*.png")))

sim/plant.h 154 satır
// Iki tekerlekli ters sarkac + DC motor + sensor modeli. Yalniz PC simulasyonu icin.
#pragma once
#include <cmath>
#include <random>

#include "cdtp_config.h"
#include "plant_nominal.h"
#include "robot_logic.h"

struct PlantParams {
  double m_body = PlantNominal::m_body, l_com = PlantNominal::l_com, I_body = PlantNominal::I_body;
  double m_wheel = PlantNominal::m_wheel, r = PlantNominal::r, J_rotor = PlantNominal::J_rotor, gear = PlantNominal::gear;
  double Ke = PlantNominal::Ke, R = PlantNominal::R, b_visc = PlantNominal::b_visc, tau_c = PlantNominal::tau_coulomb;
  double track = PlantNominal::track, I_yaw = PlantNominal::I_yaw;
  double voc = 12.0, r_int = 0.15;   // paket bos gerilim, ic direnc
  double box_mass = 0.06, box_h = 0.2065;  // ilac kutusu (eksenden yukseklik)
  double h_imu = 0.05;               // IMU'nun eksenden yuksekligi
  double imu_offset_deg = 0.0;       // IMU montaj hatasi
  double gyro_bias_dps = 0.0;        // kalibrasyon sonrasi kalan bias
  double gyro_noise_dps = 0.05, acc_noise = 0.03;  // rms
  double vib_acc = 0.3;              // motor titresimi (m/s^2 rms, motor gerilimiyle olcekli)
  double backlash_deg = 0.0;         // (kullanilmiyor) disli boslugu
};

class Plant {
 public:
  explicit Plant(const PlantParams& p, unsigned seed = 1) : p_(p), rng_(seed) {
    // kutu govdeye eklenir: toplam kutle ve kutle merkezi
    mb_ = p.m_body + p.box_mass;
    l_ = (p.m_body * p.l_com + p.box_mass * p.box_h) / mb_;
    Ib_ = p.I_body + p.m_body * (p.l_com - l_) * (p.l_com - l_) + p.box_mass * (p.box_h - l_) * (p.box_h - l_);
  }

  // durum
  double x = 0, v = 0, th = 0, om = 0, psi = 0, r = 0;  // r: sapma hizi
  double X = 0, Y = 0;                                  // dunya konumu
  double xl = 0, xr = 0;                                 // teker yollari
  double iL = 0, iR = 0, vbat = 12.0;
  bool lying = false;

  // dt boyunca motor gerilimleriyle ilerle (enable=false: surucu yuksek empedans)
  void step(double dutyL, double dutyR, bool enable, double dt) {
    const double r_ = p_.r;
    const double Iw = 2 * 0.5 * p_.m_wheel * r_ * r_;
    const double Jm = 2 * p_.J_rotor * p_.gear * p_.gear;
    const double vl = v - r * p_.track / 2, vr = v + r * p_.track / 2;
    const double wl = vl / r_ - om, wr = vr / r_ - om;  // bagil mil hizi
    auto torque = [&](double duty, double w, double& cur) {
      double t;
      if (enable) {
        double V = duty * vbat;
        cur = (V - p_.Ke * w) / p_.R;
        t = p_.Ke * cur;
      } else {
        cur = 0;
        t = 0;
      }
      return t - p_.b_visc * w - p_.tau_c * std::tanh(w / 0.3);
    };
    const double tl = torque(dutyL, wl, iL), tr = torque(dutyR, wr, iR);
    vbat = p_.voc - p_.r_int * (std::fabs(iL) + std::fabs(iR));
    const double tau = tl + tr;

    if (lying) {  // yere dustu: tekerler yerinde
      v *= 0.9;
      om = 0;
      r = 0;
      return;
    }
    const double a = 2 * p_.m_wheel * r_ * r_ + Iw + mb_ * r_ * r_ + Jm;
    const double c = mb_ * l_ * l_ + Ib_ + Jm;
    const double bc = mb_ * r_ * l_ * std::cos(th) - Jm;
    const double f1 = tau + mb_ * r_ * l_ * std::sin(th) * om * om;
    const double f2 = -tau + mb_ * 9.81 * l_ * std::sin(th);
    const double det = a * c - bc * bc;
    const double phidd = (c * f1 - bc * f2) / det;
    const double thdd = (a * f2 - bc * f1) / det;
    // sapma
    const double Iy = p_.I_yaw + (p_.track * p_.track / (4 * r_ * r_)) * (Iw + Jm);
    const double rd = ((p_.track / (2 * r_)) * (tr - tl) - 0.02 * r) / Iy;

    v += r_ * phidd * dt;
    om += thdd * dt;
    r += rd * dt;
    x += v * dt;
    th += om * dt;
    psi += r * dt;
    xl += (v - r * p_.track / 2) * dt;
    xr += (v + r * p_.track / 2) * dt;
    X += v * std::cos(psi) * dt;
    Y += v * std::sin(psi) * dt;
    accX_ = r_ * phidd;
    thdd_ = thdd;
    if (std::fabs(th) > 80 * M_PI / 180) lying = true;
  }

  // IMU olcumu (ham eksen, MPU6050 X ileri / Y sol / Z yukari): acc[0]=ileri, acc[2]=yukari, gyro[1]=+yunuslama, gyro[2]=sapma
  cdtp::ImuSample imu(bool motorsOn) {
    std::normal_distribution<double> n(0, 1);
    const double h = p_.h_imu;
    // IMU noktasinin ivmesi (dunya: ileri, yukari)
    const double ax = accX_ + h * (thdd_ * std::cos(th) - om * om * std::sin(th));
    const double az = h * (-thdd_ * std::sin(th) - om * om * std::cos(th));
    const double fx = ax, fz = az + 9.81;
    const double thm = th + p_.imu_offset_deg * M_PI / 180;
    double fwd = fx * std::cos(thm) - fz * std::sin(thm);
    double up = fx * std::sin(thm) + fz * std::cos(thm);
    const double vib = motorsOn ? p_.vib_acc * (0.3 + std::fabs(iL + iR) / 2) : 0;
    fwd += (p_.acc_noise + vib) * n(rng_);
    up += (p_.acc_noise + vib) * n(rng_);
    double side = (p_.acc_noise + vib) * n(rng_);
    const double gn = p_.gyro_noise_dps * M_PI / 180;
    double gp = om + p_.gyro_bias_dps * M_PI / 180 + gn * n(rng_);
    double gy = r + gn * n(rng_);
    // MPU6050 DLPF (~42 Hz) birinci derece
    const double a = 1.0 - std::exp(-2 * M_PI * 42.0 / 200.0);
    f_[0] += a * (fwd - f_[0]);
    f_[1] += a * (side - f_[1]);
    f_[2] += a * (up - f_[2]);
    f_[3] += a * (gp - f_[3]);
    f_[4] += a * (gy - f_[4]);
    cdtp::ImuSample s;
    s.acc[0] = (float)f_[0];
    s.acc[1] = (float)f_[1];
    s.acc[2] = (float)f_[2];
    s.gyro[0] = 0;
    s.gyro[1] = (float)f_[3];
    s.gyro[2] = (float)f_[4];
    return s;
  }

  void initFilter() {
    f_[0] = -9.81 * std::sin(th);
    f_[2] = 9.81 * std::cos(th);
  }

  int32_t encCount(bool right) const {
    const double mpc = 2 * M_PI * cdtp::cfg::WHEEL_RADIUS_M / cdtp::cfg::ENC_COUNTS_PER_REV;
    // gercek enkoder teker ile govde arasindaki bagil aciyi sayar: yol - r*theta
    double c = std::floor(((right ? xr : xl) - p_.r * th) / mpc);
    bool inv = right ? cdtp::cfg::ENC_RIGHT_INVERT : cdtp::cfg::ENC_LEFT_INVERT;
    return (int32_t)(inv ? -c : c);
  }

  const PlantParams& params() const { return p_; }

 private:
  PlantParams p_;
  std::mt19937 rng_;
  double mb_, l_, Ib_;
  double accX_ = 0, thdd_ = 0;
  double f_[5] = {0, 0, 9.81, 0, 0};
};
sim/harness.h 193 satır
// Simulasyon duzenegi: fiziksel model + istasyon IR isareti + eldiven paketleri + robot mantigi.
#pragma once
#include <cstdio>
#include <functional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "plant.h"
#include "robot_logic.h"

struct GloveEvent {
  double t;
  cdtp::Cmd cmd;  // bu andan itibaren eldivenin yolladigi komut
};

struct Trace {
  double t, x, v, th, psi, X, Y, dutyL, dutyR;
  int mode, dock, nL, nR;
};

class Harness {
 public:
  Harness(const PlantParams& pp, unsigned seed = 1) : plant(pp, seed), rng_(seed * 7919u + 13) {}

  Plant plant;
  cdtp::RobotLogic logic;
  cdtp::RobotOutputs out;
  std::vector<Trace> trace;
  bool record = false;

  // istasyon: dunya orijininde, yuzu +X'e bakar
  bool stationOn = false;
  bool docked = false;       // plakalar pogo'ya degiyor
  bool hitWallBad = false;   // istasyona yanlis aciyla carpti
  double dockTime = -1, chargeStart = -1;
  double frontOffset = 0.06;  // robot merkezinden on yuze
  double eyeAxisDeg = 15;     // gozlerin disa bakis acisi (+-)
  double eyeHalfDeg = 30;     // goz tupunun kabul yari acisi

  // eldiven
  std::vector<GloveEvent> glove;
  bool gloveOn = false;
  double gloveLoss = 0.03;    // paket kaybi orani
  double gloveCutFrom = 1e9, gloveCutTo = -1;  // bu aralikta baglanti yok

  bool motorPower = true;  // acil stop sonrasi hat
  double t = 0;

  void run(double duration, const std::function<void(Harness&)>& each = nullptr) {
    const int sub = 10;  // fizik 2 kHz
    const double dtc = cdtp::cfg::DT, dtp = dtc / sub;
    const double tEnd = t + duration;
    while (t < tEnd - 1e-9) {
      // --- sensorler ---
      cdtp::RobotInputs in;
      in.t = (float)t;
      in.imu = plant.imu(out.motorEnable);
      in.encL = plant.encCount(false);
      in.encR = plant.encCount(true);
      in.vbat = (float)(plant.vbat + noise() * 0.02);
      in.vcharge = docked ? 12.4f : 0.0f;
      in.irL = irCount_[0];
      in.irR = irCount_[1];
      in.motorPower = motorPower;
      gloveInput(in);
      out = logic.step(in);
      if (logic.mode() == cdtp::Mode::CHARGING && docked && chargeStart < 0) chargeStart = t;
      // --- fizik ---
      double dl = out.dutyL * (cdtp::cfg::MOTOR_LEFT_INVERT ? -1 : 1);
      double dr = out.dutyR * (cdtp::cfg::MOTOR_RIGHT_INVERT ? -1 : 1);
      dl = std::round(dl * 1023) / 1023;  // 10 bit PWM
      dr = std::round(dr * 1023) / 1023;
      for (int i = 0; i < sub; ++i) {
        plant.step(dl, dr, out.motorEnable && motorPower, dtp);
        t += dtp;
        beacon(t);
        stationContact();
      }
      if (record) {
        trace.push_back({t, plant.x, plant.v, plant.th, plant.psi, plant.X, plant.Y, out.dutyL, out.dutyR,
                         (int)logic.mode(), (int)logic.dockState(), logic.irNL(), logic.irNR()});
      }
      if (each) each(*this);
    }
  }

  void writeCsv(const std::string& path) const {
    FILE* f = std::fopen(path.c_str(), "w");
    if (!f) return;
    std::fprintf(f, "t,x,v,theta_deg,psi_deg,X,Y,dutyL,dutyR,mode,dock,nL,nR\n");
    for (const auto& s : trace)
      std::fprintf(f, "%.4f,%.4f,%.4f,%.3f,%.2f,%.4f,%.4f,%.3f,%.3f,%d,%d,%d,%d\n", s.t, s.x, s.v,
                   s.th * 180 / M_PI, s.psi * 180 / M_PI, s.X, s.Y, s.dutyL, s.dutyR, s.mode, s.dock, s.nL, s.nR);
    std::fclose(f);
  }

 private:
  std::mt19937 rng_;
  int32_t irCount_[2] = {0, 0};
  double nextBurst_ = 0;
  uint16_t seq_ = 0;
  double nextPacket_ = 0;

  double noise() {
    std::normal_distribution<double> n(0, 1);
    return n(rng_);
  }
  double uni() {
    std::uniform_real_distribution<double> u(0, 1);
    return u(rng_);
  }

  // Istasyon isareti: 78 ms cerceve, ilk 45 ms'de ~1 kHz patlama (44 adet), sonra 33 ms sessizlik
  void beacon(double now) {
    if (!stationOn) return;
    while (nextBurst_ <= now) {
      const double phase = std::fmod(nextBurst_, 0.078);
      if (phase < 0.045) {
        for (int e = 0; e < 2; ++e)
          if (uni() < eyeGain(e)) irCount_[e]++;
      }
      nextBurst_ += 0.0010122;
      if (std::fmod(nextBurst_, 0.078) >= 0.045) nextBurst_ = (std::floor(nextBurst_ / 0.078) + 1) * 0.078;
    }
  }

  // goz kazanci: 0..1 (patlamayi algilama olasiligi)
  double eyeGain(int eye) const {
    const double fx = plant.X + frontOffset * std::cos(plant.psi), fy = plant.Y + frontOffset * std::sin(plant.psi);
    const double dist = std::hypot(fx, fy);
    if (dist > 1.2 || fx < -0.01) return 0;
    const double gamma = std::atan2(fy, fx);  // istasyon normaline gore robotun acisi
    const double emit = std::max(0.0, 1 - std::pow(gamma / (70 * M_PI / 180), 2));
    double bearing = std::atan2(-fy, -fx) - plant.psi;  // istasyonun robota gore yonu, sola +
    while (bearing > M_PI) bearing -= 2 * M_PI;
    while (bearing < -M_PI) bearing += 2 * M_PI;
    const double axis = (eye == 0 ? eyeAxisDeg : -eyeAxisDeg) * M_PI / 180;
    const double acc = std::max(0.0, 1 - std::pow((bearing - axis) / (eyeHalfDeg * M_PI / 180), 2));
    const double g = emit * acc;
    if (g < 0.12) return 0;  // alici esigi
    return std::min(1.0, g * 1.4);
  }

  // Istasyon: orijinde dikey yuvarlak direk (cap 40 mm), uzerinde iki bakir halka (ust +, alt -) ve IR isaret.
  // Robotun onunde V-yuva var; yuvanin icindeki iki yayli pim halkalara degir. Direk yuvarlak oldugu icin
  // yaklasma acisi onemsizdir; V-yuva yanal +-3 cm hatayi kendisi ortalar.
  void stationContact() {
    if (!stationOn) return;
    const double fx = plant.X + frontOffset * std::cos(plant.psi), fy = plant.Y + frontOffset * std::sin(plant.psi);
    // direk merkezinin robot eksenine gore konumu
    const double dx = -fx, dy = -fy;
    const double along = dx * std::cos(plant.psi) + dy * std::sin(plant.psi);
    const double lateral = -dx * std::sin(plant.psi) + dy * std::cos(plant.psi);
    const double postR = 0.02;
    const bool touching = along <= postR + 0.003 && along > -0.05 && std::fabs(lateral) < 0.09;
    if (touching) {
      if (std::fabs(lateral) < 0.03) {
        if (!docked) dockTime = t;
        docked = true;
      } else {
        hitWallBad = true;  // yuvanin disindan, govde kenariyla carpti
      }
      if (plant.th > 6 * M_PI / 180) {  // direge yaslanir
        plant.th = 6 * M_PI / 180;
        if (plant.om > 0) plant.om = 0;
      }
      if (plant.v > 0) plant.v = 0;  // direk ileri gitmeyi engeller
    } else if (docked && along > postR + 0.01) {
      docked = false;
    }
  }

  void gloveInput(cdtp::RobotInputs& in) {
    in.gloveNew = false;
    if (!gloveOn || t < nextPacket_) return;
    nextPacket_ = t + 0.05;  // 20 Hz
    if (t >= gloveCutFrom && t <= gloveCutTo) return;
    if (uni() < gloveLoss) return;
    cdtp::Cmd c = cdtp::Cmd::STOP;
    for (const auto& e : glove)
      if (e.t <= t) c = e.cmd;
    cdtp::GlovePacket p{};
    p.cmd = (uint8_t)c;
    p.seq = seq_++;
    cdtp::sealPacket(p);
    cdtp::GlovePacket chk;
    if (cdtp::checkPacket(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(&p), sizeof(p), chk)) {
      in.gloveNew = true;
      in.glove = chk;
    }
  }
};
sim/sim_main.cpp 411 satır
// Sartnamedeki kabul testlerinin simulasyonu. Robot ESP32'sindeki ayni RobotLogic kodu kosar.
// Derle: tools/run_sim.sh   Cikti: sim/results/
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "harness.h"
#include "unit_tests.h"

using cdtp::Cmd;
using cdtp::Mode;

static const double D2R = M_PI / 180.0, R2D = 180.0 / M_PI;
static std::string OUT = "sim/results/";

struct Result {
  std::string id, name;
  bool pass;
  std::string detail;
};
static std::vector<Result> results;
static double g_lastD = 0, g_lastDx = 0, g_lastTh = 0;

static void report(const std::string& id, const std::string& name, bool pass, const char* fmt, ...) {
  char buf[512];
  va_list ap;
  va_start(ap, fmt);
  std::vsnprintf(buf, sizeof buf, fmt, ap);
  va_end(ap);
  results.push_back({id, name, pass, buf});
  std::printf("[%s] %-6s %-44s %s\n", pass ? "GECTI" : "KALDI", id.c_str(), name.c_str(), buf);
}

// Robotu dik baslatip dengeye aldirir (elle dik tutup birakma)
static void arm(Harness& h) {
  h.plant.initFilter();
  h.run(1.0);
}

struct Stats {
  double maxTh = 0, maxX = 0, minX = 1e9, maxXabs = 0;
  bool fell = false;
  void operator()(Harness& h) {
    maxTh = std::max(maxTh, std::fabs(h.plant.th));
    maxX = std::max(maxX, h.plant.x);
    minX = std::min(minX, h.plant.x);
    maxXabs = std::max(maxXabs, std::fabs(h.plant.x));
    if (h.logic.mode() == Mode::FALLEN || h.plant.lying) fell = true;
  }
};

// ---------- C1: denge ve temel hareket ----------
static bool testStatic(const PlantParams& pp, unsigned seed, bool rep, double dur = 30) {
  Harness h(pp, seed);
  h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::STATIC);
  h.record = rep;
  arm(h);
  Stats s;
  h.run(dur, std::ref(s));
  bool ok = !s.fell && h.logic.mode() == Mode::BALANCE && s.maxTh < 5 * D2R && s.maxXabs < 0.15;
  if (rep) {
    h.writeCsv(OUT + "c1_statik.csv");
    report("C1.1", "30 s statik denge (kutulu)", ok, "en buyuk egim %.2f deg, en buyuk kayma %.1f cm", s.maxTh * R2D,
           s.maxXabs * 100);
  }
  return ok;
}

static bool testMove1m(const PlantParams& pp, unsigned seed, bool rep, bool forward) {
  Harness h(pp, seed);
  h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::GUIDANCE);
  h.gloveOn = true;
  h.record = rep;
  Cmd c = forward ? Cmd::FWD : Cmd::BACK;
  // robot once 5 s dengede bekler (denge acisini ogrenir), sonra iki adim komutu: 0,5 + 0,5 m
  h.glove = {{0, Cmd::STOP}, {6.0, c}, {6.4, Cmd::STOP}, {9.5, c}, {9.9, Cmd::STOP}};
  arm(h);
  Stats s;
  h.run(13.0, std::ref(s));
  // 3 s durma
  double x0 = h.plant.x, maxV = 0, maxTh = 0, maxDx = 0;
  h.run(3.0, [&](Harness& hh) {
    maxV = std::max(maxV, std::fabs(hh.plant.v));
    maxTh = std::max(maxTh, std::fabs(hh.plant.th));
    maxDx = std::max(maxDx, std::fabs(hh.plant.x - x0));
  });
  double d = std::fabs(h.plant.x);
  // "durdu": 3 s boyunca +-3 cm icinde (disli surtunmesi + sensor gurultusu kucuk bir titresim birakir)
  g_lastD = d;
  g_lastDx = maxDx;
  g_lastTh = s.maxTh;
  bool ok = !s.fell && d > 0.9 && d < 1.1 && maxDx < 0.03;
  if (rep) {
    h.writeCsv(OUT + (forward ? "c1_ileri.csv" : "c1_geri.csv"));
    report(forward ? "C1.2" : "C1.3", forward ? "1 m ileri + 3 s dur" : "1 m geri + 3 s dur", ok,
           "yol %.3f m, 3 s durmada en buyuk kayma %.1f mm (hiz tepe %.3f m/s), en buyuk egim %.1f deg", d,
           maxDx * 1000, maxV, s.maxTh * R2D);
  }
  return ok;
}

static bool testRecovery(const PlantParams& pp, unsigned seed, bool rep, double deg, double* tRec = nullptr,
                         double* travel = nullptr) {
  Harness h(pp, seed);
  h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::STATIC);
  h.record = rep;
  arm(h);
  // elle +-10 deg egilip birakildi
  h.plant.th = deg * D2R;
  h.plant.om = 0;
  double t0 = h.t, recovered = -1, maxDx = 0, maxAfter = 0;
  double x0 = h.plant.x;
  Stats s;
  // toparlanma: |theta| < 3 deg ve |v| < 0,1 m/s'ye ilk varis; sonrasinda egim 5 deg'yi hic asmamali
  h.run(10.0, [&](Harness& hh) {
    s(hh);
    maxDx = std::max(maxDx, std::fabs(hh.plant.x - x0));
    if (recovered < 0 && std::fabs(hh.plant.th) < 3 * D2R && std::fabs(hh.plant.v) < 0.1) recovered = hh.t - t0;
    if (recovered >= 0) maxAfter = std::max(maxAfter, std::fabs(hh.plant.th));
  });
  if (maxAfter > 5 * D2R) recovered = -1;
  bool ok = !s.fell && recovered >= 0 && recovered < 10;
  if (tRec) *tRec = recovered;
  if (travel) *travel = maxDx;
  if (rep) h.writeCsv(OUT + (deg > 0 ? "c1_toparlanma_arti.csv" : "c1_toparlanma_eksi.csv"));
  return ok;
}

// ---------- On test: enerjisizken pasif kararsizlik ----------
static void testPassive() {
  bool ok = true;
  double worst = 0;
  for (double deg : {10.0, -10.0}) {
    Harness h(PlantParams(), 3);
    h.plant.initFilter();
    h.plant.th = deg * D2R;
    double tFall = -1;
    // motorlar enerjisiz: mantigi hic calistirmadan yalniz fizik
    for (int i = 0; i < 5 * 2000 && !h.plant.lying; ++i) h.plant.step(0, 0, false, 0.0005);
    tFall = h.plant.lying ? 0 : -1;
    double tt = 0;
    Harness h2(PlantParams(), 3);
    h2.plant.th = deg * D2R;
    while (tt < 5 && std::fabs(h2.plant.th) < 40 * D2R) {
      h2.plant.step(0, 0, false, 0.0005);
      tt += 0.0005;
    }
    if (tt >= 5) ok = false;
    worst = std::max(worst, tt);
    (void)tFall;
  }
  report("ON", "Enerjisiz +-10 deg'den dik kalmamali", ok, "40 deg'yi gecme suresi en fazla %.2f s (sinir: 5 s icinde dusmeli)",
         worst);
}

// ---------- C2: eldiven komutlari ----------
static void testGlove() {
  struct Case { const char* id; const char* name; Cmd c; bool lin; double sign; };
  Case cs[] = {{"C2.1", "Eldiven: ileri ~0,5 m", Cmd::FWD, true, 1},
               {"C2.2", "Eldiven: geri ~0,5 m", Cmd::BACK, true, -1},
               {"C2.3", "Eldiven: sola ~90 deg", Cmd::LEFT, false, 1},
               {"C2.4", "Eldiven: saga ~90 deg", Cmd::RIGHT, false, -1}};
  for (auto& k : cs) {
    Harness h(PlantParams(), 11);
    h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::GUIDANCE);
    h.gloveOn = true;
    h.gloveLoss = 0.05;
    h.glove = {{0, Cmd::STOP}, {1.5, k.c}, {2.0, Cmd::STOP}};
    arm(h);
    const double tCmd = 1.5;  // eldiven jesti bu anda baslar
    double tResp = -1, x0 = h.plant.x;
    Stats s;
    h.run(6.0, [&](Harness& hh) {
      s(hh);
      if (hh.t <= tCmd) x0 = hh.plant.x;
      // tepki: komut yonunde 3 cm yol ya da 5 deg donus (denge titremesi tetiklemesin)
      bool moving = k.lin ? (hh.plant.x - x0) * k.sign > 0.03 : hh.plant.psi * k.sign > 5 * D2R;
      if (moving && tResp < 0 && hh.t > tCmd) tResp = hh.t - tCmd;
    });
    double val = k.lin ? h.plant.x : h.plant.psi * R2D;
    bool ok = !s.fell && tResp >= 0 && tResp < 2.0 &&
              (k.lin ? (std::fabs(val - 0.5 * k.sign) < 0.1) : (std::fabs(val - 90 * k.sign) < 15));
    report(k.id, k.name, ok, k.lin ? "tepki %.2f s, yol %.3f m" : "tepki %.2f s, donus %.1f deg", tResp, val);
  }
  // dur: bes komut art arda, sonra dur; robot durur ve yerinde kalir
  {
    Harness h(PlantParams(), 12);
    h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::GUIDANCE);
    h.gloveOn = true;
    h.gloveLoss = 0.05;
    h.record = true;
    h.glove = {{0, Cmd::STOP},  {1.5, Cmd::FWD},    {1.9, Cmd::STOP},  {5.0, Cmd::BACK}, {5.4, Cmd::STOP},
               {8.5, Cmd::LEFT}, {8.9, Cmd::STOP}, {11.0, Cmd::RIGHT}, {11.4, Cmd::STOP}};
    arm(h);
    Stats s;
    h.run(13.5, std::ref(s));
    double x0 = h.plant.x, p0 = h.plant.psi;
    h.run(3.0, std::ref(s));
    bool ok = !s.fell && std::fabs(h.plant.x - x0) < 0.03 && std::fabs(h.plant.psi - p0) < 3 * D2R;
    h.writeCsv(OUT + "c2_eldiven.csv");
    report("C2.5", "Dur: tum komutlardan sonra yerinde kalir", ok, "son 3 s kayma %.1f mm, donme %.2f deg",
           std::fabs(h.plant.x - x0) * 1000, std::fabs(h.plant.psi - p0) * R2D);
  }
  // baglanti kopmasi: adim ortasinda eldiven susarsa robot durur
  {
    Harness h(PlantParams(), 13);
    h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::GUIDANCE);
    h.gloveOn = true;
    h.glove = {{0, Cmd::STOP}, {1.5, Cmd::FWD}, {1.9, Cmd::STOP}};
    h.gloveCutFrom = 2.5;
    h.gloveCutTo = 99;
    arm(h);
    Stats s;
    h.run(5.0, std::ref(s));
    bool ok = !s.fell && std::fabs(h.plant.v) < 0.1 && h.plant.x < 0.45;
    report("GUV.1", "Eldiven sustu (0,3 s): adim kesilir, robot durur", ok, "durdugu yol %.3f m (tam adim 0,5 m)",
           h.plant.x);
  }
}

// ---------- C3: otonom yanasma ----------
struct DockRes { bool ok; double tDock, tCharge; bool bad; };

static DockRes runDock(double rho, double alphaDeg, double headOffDeg, unsigned seed, bool rec = false,
                       const PlantParams& pp = PlantParams()) {
  Harness h(pp, seed);
  h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::CHARGING);
  h.stationOn = true;
  h.record = rec;
  const double a = alphaDeg * D2R;
  h.plant.X = rho * std::cos(a);
  h.plant.Y = rho * std::sin(a);
  h.plant.psi = std::atan2(-h.plant.Y, -h.plant.X) + headOffDeg * D2R;  // istasyona bakis + sapma
  double psiW0 = h.plant.psi;
  arm(h);
  (void)psiW0;
  double tStart = h.t;
  bool fell = false;
  h.run(60.0, [&](Harness& hh) {
    if (hh.plant.lying || hh.logic.mode() == Mode::FALLEN) fell = true;
    if (hh.logic.mode() == Mode::CHARGING && hh.chargeStart > 0 && hh.t > hh.chargeStart + 0.5) hh.t = 1e9;
  });
  DockRes r;
  r.ok = !fell && h.docked && h.logic.mode() == Mode::CHARGING && !h.hitWallBad;
  r.tDock = h.dockTime > 0 ? h.dockTime - tStart : -1;
  r.tCharge = (h.chargeStart > 0 && h.dockTime > 0) ? h.chargeStart - h.dockTime : -1;
  r.bad = h.hitWallBad;
  if (rec) h.writeCsv(OUT + "c3_yanasma.csv");
  return r;
}

static void testDock() {
  int n = 0, okN = 0;
  double worstT = 0, worstCharge = 0;
  std::string fails;
  for (double rho : {0.15, 0.22, 0.30})
    for (double al : {-45.0, -30.0, -15.0, 0.0, 15.0, 30.0, 45.0})
      for (double ho : {-30.0, 0.0, 30.0}) {
        DockRes r = runDock(rho, al, ho, 100 + n);
        ++n;
        if (r.ok) {
          ++okN;
          worstT = std::max(worstT, r.tDock);
          worstCharge = std::max(worstCharge, r.tCharge);
        } else {
          char b[96];
          std::snprintf(b, sizeof b, " (%.2f m, %.0f deg, bakis %+.0f%s)", rho, al, ho, r.bad ? ", yuva disi carpma" : "");
          fails += b;
        }
      }
  report("C3.1-2", "IR ile bul, hizalan, yerles (63 baslangic, 30 cm/90 deg)", okN == n, "%d/%d basarili, en uzun %.1f s%s", okN, n,
         worstT, fails.c_str());
  report("C3.3", "Temastan sonra sarj + motor durur <= 5 s", okN > 0 && worstCharge < 5.0,
         "temas -> SARJ modu en fazla %.2f s", worstCharge);
  // arkasi istasyona donuk baslangic: ARA durumu tam tur atmali
  int backOk = 0;
  for (double ho : {90.0, -90.0, 180.0}) backOk += runDock(0.25, 10, ho, 300 + (int)ho).ok;
  report("C3.x", "Istasyon gorus disindayken arayip bulma", backOk == 3, "%d/3 basarili (90, -90, 180 deg ters bakis)",
         backOk);
  runDock(0.30, 35, 25, 7, true);
}

// ---------- C5: butunlesik gorev ----------
static void testIntegrated(int rep, bool record) {
  Harness h(PlantParams(), 500 + rep);
  h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::INTEGRATED);
  h.stationOn = true;
  h.gloveOn = true;
  h.gloveLoss = 0.05;
  h.record = record;
  // istasyon (0,0) +X'e bakar. Robot (0.27,-1.0)'dan +Y'ye bakarak baslar.
  h.plant.X = 0.27;
  h.plant.Y = -1.0;
  h.plant.psi = M_PI / 2;
  // 5 s denge -> 2 x ileri (1 m, hedef nokta) -> hedefte 5 s bekle -> sola don -> dur -> otonom yanasma
  h.glove = {{0, Cmd::STOP}, {5.0, Cmd::FWD}, {5.4, Cmd::STOP}, {8.6, Cmd::FWD}, {9.0, Cmd::STOP},
             {17.0, Cmd::LEFT}, {17.4, Cmd::STOP}};
  arm(h);
  bool fell = false, earlyDock = false;
  double targetWaitStart = -1;
  h.run(70.0, [&](Harness& hh) {
    if (hh.plant.lying || hh.logic.mode() == Mode::FALLEN) fell = true;
    if (hh.t < 17.0 && hh.logic.mode() == Mode::DOCK) earlyDock = true;
    if (targetWaitStart < 0 && hh.t > 9 && std::fabs(hh.plant.v) < 0.02) targetWaitStart = hh.t;
    if (hh.logic.mode() == Mode::CHARGING && hh.chargeStart > 0 && hh.t > hh.chargeStart + 0.5) hh.t = 1e9;
  });
  bool ok = !fell && !earlyDock && h.docked && h.logic.mode() == Mode::CHARGING;
  if (record) h.writeCsv(OUT + "c5_butunlesik.csv");
  char nm[64];
  std::snprintf(nm, sizeof nm, "Butunlesik gorev, tekrar %d", rep);
  report("C5." + std::to_string(rep), nm, ok, "hedefte bekleme %.1f s, erken yanasma %s, sarj %s, temas->sarj %.2f s",
         17.0 - targetWaitStart, earlyDock ? "VAR" : "yok", h.docked ? "basladi" : "YOK",
         h.chargeStart > 0 ? h.chargeStart - h.dockTime : -1.0);
}

// ---------- Saglamlik: parametre belirsizligi ----------
static void testRobust() {
  std::mt19937 g(2026);
  auto U = [&](double a, double b) { return std::uniform_real_distribution<double>(a, b)(g); };
  const int N = 40;
  int okS = 0, okR = 0, okM = 0, okM5 = 0;
  double worstRec = 0;
  std::string worst;
  for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
    PlantParams p;
    p.m_body *= U(0.75, 1.3);
    p.l_com *= U(0.75, 1.6);  // kutle merkezi: tahminin %75'i ile %160'i arasi
    p.I_body *= U(0.7, 1.4);
    p.R *= U(0.8, 1.25);
    p.Ke *= U(0.9, 1.1);
    p.tau_c *= U(0.5, 2.0);
    p.b_visc *= U(0.5, 2.0);
    p.voc = U(10.4, 12.6);
    p.box_mass = U(0.03, 0.10);
    p.imu_offset_deg = U(-1.5, 1.5);
    p.gyro_bias_dps = U(-0.5, 0.5);
    p.h_imu = U(0.0, 0.12);
    unsigned seed = 1000 + i;
    bool s = testStatic(p, seed, false, 15);
    double tr1 = 0, tr2 = 0;
    bool r = testRecovery(p, seed, false, 10, &tr1) && testRecovery(p, seed, false, -10, &tr2);
    bool m3 = testMove1m(p, seed, false, true);
    // modelden uzak robotlarda durma olcutu +-5 cm (nominal testte +-3 cm)
    bool m = g_lastD > 0.9 && g_lastD < 1.1 && g_lastDx < 0.05;
    okS += s;
    okR += r;
    okM += m3;
    okM5 += m;
    if (r) worstRec = std::max(worstRec, std::max(tr1, tr2));
    if (!(s && r && m)) {
      char b[160];
      std::snprintf(b, sizeof b, " [m=%.2f l=%.3f R=%.1f V=%.1f ofs=%.1f: %s%s%s d=%.2f dx=%.0fmm th=%.0f]", p.m_body,
                    p.l_com, p.R, p.voc, p.imu_offset_deg, s ? "" : "S", r ? "" : "T", m ? "" : "H", g_lastD,
                    g_lastDx * 1000, g_lastTh * R2D);
      worst += b;
    }
  }
  report("SAG", "Belirsizlik: kutle +-25%, KM -25/+60%, ...", okS == N && okR == N && okM5 == N,
         "statik %d/%d, toparlanma %d/%d (en uzun %.1f s), 1 m: +-5 cm durmada %d/%d, +-3 cm durmada %d/%d%s", okS, N,
         okR, N, worstRec, okM5, N, okM, N, worst.c_str());
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
  if (argc > 1) OUT = argv[1];
  std::printf("== Birim testleri ==\n");
  int fails = runUnitTests();
  std::printf("\n== Kabul testleri (simulasyon) ==\n");
  testPassive();
  testStatic(PlantParams(), 1, true);
  testMove1m(PlantParams(), 2, true, true);
  testMove1m(PlantParams(), 3, true, false);
  {
    double tp, tm, dp, dm;
    bool a = testRecovery(PlantParams(), 4, true, 10, &tp, &dp);
    bool b = testRecovery(PlantParams(), 5, true, -10, &tm, &dm);
    report("C1.4", "+10 ve -10 deg'den 10 s icinde toparlanma", a && b,
           "+10: %.2f s (yol %.0f cm), -10: %.2f s (yol %.0f cm)", tp, dp * 100, tm, dm * 100);
  }
  {
    Harness h(PlantParams(), 21);
    h.logic.setTask(cdtp::Task::STATIC);
    arm(h);
    h.motorPower = false;  // acil stopa basildi
    h.run(0.02);
    bool off = h.logic.mode() == Mode::STANDBY && !h.out.motorEnable;
    h.motorPower = true;   // birakildi; robot yatik -> kendiliginden donmemeli
    h.run(0.3);
    bool stay = h.logic.mode() != Mode::BALANCE || std::fabs(h.plant.th) < 3 * D2R;
    report("GUV.2", "Acil stop: denge birakilir, motorlar kapanir", off && stay, "stop sonrasi mod %s, bekleme %s",
           off ? "BEKLE" : "YANLIS", stay ? "dogru" : "yanlis");
  }
  testGlove();
  testDock();
  for (int i = 1; i <= 4; ++i) testIntegrated(i, i == 1);
  testRobust();

  int nOk = 0;
  for (auto& r : results) nOk += r.pass;
  std::printf("\nOZET: %d/%d kabul testi gecti, birim testi hatasi: %d\n", nOk, (int)results.size(), fails);

  FILE* f = std::fopen((OUT + "summary.tsv").c_str(), "w");
  if (f) {
    for (auto& r : results) std::fprintf(f, "%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", r.id.c_str(), r.name.c_str(), r.pass ? "GECTI" : "KALDI", r.detail.c_str());
    std::fclose(f);
  }
  return (nOk == (int)results.size() && fails == 0) ? 0 : 1;
}
sim/unit_tests.h 101 satır
// Ortak cekirdek icin birim testleri (eldiven jesti, IR cerceve, paket, profil, telemetri).
#pragma once
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

#include "gesture.h"
#include "ir_frame.h"
#include "protocol.h"
#include "robot_logic.h"

static int g_fail = 0;
#define EXPECT(c, msg)                                   \
  do {                                                   \
    if (!(c)) {                                          \
      std::printf("  HATA: %s (%s:%d)\n", msg, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
      ++g_fail;                                          \
    }                                                    \
  } while (0)

static cdtp::Cmd feed(cdtp::GestureClassifier& g, float p, float r, float sec) {
  cdtp::Cmd c = cdtp::Cmd::NONE;
  for (float t = 0; t < sec; t += 0.02f) c = g.update(p, r, 0.02f);
  return c;
}

static int runUnitTests() {
  using cdtp::Cmd;
  // --- jest siniflandirici ---
  {
    cdtp::GestureClassifier g;
    EXPECT(feed(g, 0, 0, 0.5) == Cmd::STOP, "notr el = dur");
    EXPECT(feed(g, 30, 0, 0.3) == Cmd::FWD, "one 30 deg = ileri");
    EXPECT(feed(g, 18, 0, 0.5) == Cmd::FWD, "histerezis: 18 deg'de ileri surer");
    EXPECT(feed(g, 5, 0, 0.5) == Cmd::STOP, "5 deg'de dur");
    EXPECT(feed(g, -30, 0, 0.3) == Cmd::BACK, "geri");
    feed(g, 0, 0, 0.5);
    EXPECT(feed(g, 0, -30, 0.3) == Cmd::LEFT, "sol");
    feed(g, 0, 0, 0.5);
    EXPECT(feed(g, 0, 30, 0.3) == Cmd::RIGHT, "sag");
    feed(g, 0, 0, 0.5);
    EXPECT(feed(g, 28, 26, 0.5) == Cmd::STOP, "capraz el (28/26) belirsiz: komut degismez");
    EXPECT(feed(g, 30, 0, 0.06) == Cmd::STOP, "0,06 s'lik titreme komut sayilmaz");
    EXPECT(feed(g, 20, 0, 1.0) == Cmd::STOP, "20 deg giris esiginin altinda");
  }
  // --- IR cerceve sayaci ---
  {
    cdtp::IrFrameCounter c;
    int32_t cnt = 0;
    int got = -1;
    for (int ms = 0; ms < 400; ms += 1) {
      double ph = std::fmod(ms, 78.0);
      if (ph < 45 && ms % 1 == 0) cnt++;  // ~44-45 patlama
      if (ms % 5 == 0) {
        c.sample(cnt, ms / 1000.0f);
        if (c.n() > 0) got = c.n();
      }
    }
    EXPECT(got >= 40 && got <= 46, "tam gorus ~44 darbe/cerceve");
    for (int ms = 400; ms < 800; ms += 5) c.sample(cnt, ms / 1000.0f);
    EXPECT(c.n() == 0, "isaret kesilince 0,25 s icinde n=0");
  }
  // --- paket ---
  {
    cdtp::GlovePacket p{};
    p.cmd = (uint8_t)Cmd::LEFT;
    p.seq = 77;
    cdtp::sealPacket(p);
    cdtp::GlovePacket o;
    EXPECT(cdtp::checkPacket((const uint8_t*)&p, sizeof p, o) && o.cmd == (uint8_t)Cmd::LEFT, "gecerli paket");
    uint8_t bad[sizeof p];
    std::memcpy(bad, &p, sizeof p);
    bad[5] ^= 1;
    EXPECT(!cdtp::checkPacket(bad, sizeof p, o), "bozuk CRC reddedilir");
    cdtp::GlovePacket q = p;
    q.key = 0x12345678;
    q.crc = cdtp::crc8((const uint8_t*)&q, sizeof q - 1);
    EXPECT(!cdtp::checkPacket((const uint8_t*)&q, sizeof q, o), "baska takimin anahtari reddedilir");
  }
  // --- trapez profil ---
  {
    cdtp::Trapezoid tr;
    tr.start(0.5f, 0.25f, 0.4f);
    float vmax = 0;
    int n = 0;
    while (tr.active() && n < 4000) { tr.step(0.005f); vmax = std::max(vmax, tr.vel()); ++n; }
    EXPECT(std::fabs(tr.pos() - 0.5f) < 1e-3f, "profil 0,5 m'de biter");
    EXPECT(vmax <= 0.2501f, "hiz siniri");
  }
  // --- telemetri ---
  {
    cdtp::RobotLogic lg;
    char buf[320];
    int n = lg.telemetryJson(buf, sizeof buf, 5);
    EXPECT(n > 0 && n < (int)sizeof buf && buf[0] == '{' && std::strstr(buf, "\"tilt\":") && std::strstr(buf, "\"chg\":"),
           "telemetri JSON");
    std::printf("  ornek telemetri (%d bayt): %s\n", n, buf);
  }
  std::printf("  birim testleri: %s\n", g_fail ? "HATA VAR" : "hepsi gecti");
  return g_fail;
}

MuJoCo dijital ikizi

twin/twin.py 637 satır
"""MuJoCo dijital ikizi.

C++ simulasyonundan (sim/plant.h) bagimsiz ikinci bir model:
  * Govde tools/mass_budget.py'deki her parcadan ayri kutu olarak kurulur (kutle, konum, atalet MuJoCo hesaplar).
  * Teker-zemin temasi, kayma, ilac kutusunun cepte durmasi, V-yuvanin direge oturmasi gercek temasla cozulur.
  * Sensorler fiziksel kurallarla uretilir: MPU6050 (X ileri, Y sol, Z yukari) MuJoCo'nun ivme/jiroskop
    sensorunden; enkoder teker ile govde arasindaki BAGIL acidan (gercek enkoder gibi).
  * Karar mantigi: robot firmware'indeki RobotLogic, build/twin_bridge.exe uzerinden (ayni C++ kodu).
Calistir:  <venv>/python twin/twin.py   ->  twin/results/*.csv ve summary.tsv
"""
import math
import pathlib
import subprocess
import sys

import mujoco
import numpy as np

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "tools"))
import mass_budget  # noqa: E402

OUT = ROOT / "twin/results"
OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
BRIDGE = ROOT / "build/twin_bridge.exe"

# ---- fiziksel parametreler (lqr_design.py ile ayni kaynaklar; motor degerleri veri sayfasi tahmini) ----
R_WHEEL, TRACK = 0.035, 0.19
KE, RA = 0.216, 4.5
J_ROTOR, GEAR = 5.0e-7, 19.0
B_VISC, TAU_C = 0.002, 0.02
CPR = 836
VOC, RINT = 12.0, 0.15
MAGNET_N, MAGNET_RANGE = 10.0, 0.006  # V-yuvadaki miknatis ile direkteki celik serit: temasta ~10 N, 6 mm'de sifir
KX, KY = 0.016, 0.027  # ilac kutusu cebinin konumu (on, sol)
PHYS_DT, CTRL_EVERY = 0.0005, 10  # 2 kHz fizik, 200 Hz kontrol
TASKS = {"statik": 0, "yonlendirme": 1, "sarj": 2, "butunlesik": 3}
CMD = {"yok": 0, "ileri": 1, "geri": 2, "sol": 3, "sag": 4, "dur": 5}

# parca boyutlari (yari olcu, m) mass_budget isimlerine gore
SIZES = {
    "Kat": (0.055, 0.075, 0.002),
    "BTS": (0.025, 0.030, 0.015),
    "3x18650": (0.035, 0.040, 0.012),
    "Ana kart": (0.025, 0.050, 0.008),
    "Acil": (0.020, 0.020, 0.020),
    "Kutu yuvasi": (0.038, 0.025, 0.003),
    "LM2596": (0.020, 0.030, 0.010),
    "MPU": (0.010, 0.010, 0.002),
    "IR": (0.008, 0.020, 0.010),
    "16x": (0.050, 0.070, 0.004),
}


def size_for(name):
    for k, v in SIZES.items():
        if name.startswith(k):
            return v
    return (0.02, 0.02, 0.02)


def build_xml(post_xy=None, box=True):
    geoms = []
    for name, m, z, x, _ in mass_budget.PARTS:
        if name.startswith("2x JGB37"):
            # iki motor teker ekseninde, y boyunca
            for s in (1, -1):
                geoms.append(f'<geom type="cylinder" size="0.0185 0.035" pos="0 {s*0.045} -0.007" euler="90 0 0" mass="{m/2}" rgba=".3 .5 .5 1"/>')
            continue
        sx, sy, sz = size_for(name)
        # yalniz katlar carpisir (yere dusme, direk); diger parcalar kutle/atalet tasir, kutu cebine girmez
        coll = "" if name.startswith("Kat") else ' contype="0" conaffinity="0"'
        geoms.append(f'<geom type="box" size="{sx} {sy} {sz}" pos="{x} 0 {z}" mass="{m}" rgba=".6 .7 .8 1"{coll}/>')
    # kutu cebi (kat 3 ustunde, 4 duvar, kutle kutu yuvasi parcasinda sayildi)
    pocket = []
    # cad/robot_cad.py ile ayni: kat 3 ustu z=0.194, cep ici 72x47 mm, merkez y=+0.027 (sagda acil stop)
    zc = 0.194 + 0.0125
    for px, py, hx, hy in ((KX + 0.0372, KY, 0.0012, 0.0259), (KX - 0.0372, KY, 0.0012, 0.0259),
                           (KX, KY + 0.0247, 0.0384, 0.0012), (KX, KY - 0.0247, 0.0384, 0.0012)):
        pocket.append(f'<geom type="box" size="{hx} {hy} 0.0125" pos="{px} {py} {zc}" mass="0.0001" rgba=".8 .8 .9 .5"/>')
    # V-yuva (belgedeki gibi): arkada 20 mm genis dikey serit (pimler burada, x=0.058), iki yanda 45 derece kanat
    vee = ['<geom type="box" size="0.0015 0.010 0.025" pos="0.0575 0 0.06" mass="0.0001" rgba=".2 .3 .9 1"/>']
    for s_ in (1, -1):
        # kanat (0.058, +-0.010) noktasindan 45 derece disa, 42 mm
        cx, cy = 0.058 + 0.0148, s_ * (0.010 + 0.0148)
        vee.append(f'<geom type="box" size="0.021 0.0015 0.025" pos="{cx} {cy} 0.06" euler="0 0 {s_*45}" mass="0.0001" rgba=".2 .3 .9 1"/>')
    post = ""
    if post_xy is not None:
        post = (f'<body name="post" pos="{post_xy[0]} {post_xy[1]} 0.1"><geom type="cylinder" size="0.02 0.1" '
                f'rgba=".5 .5 .5 1"/></body>')
    box_body = ""
    if box:
        box_body = (f'<body name="pillbox" pos="{KX} {KY} {R_WHEEL + zc + 0.001}"><freejoint/>'
                    f'<geom type="box" size="0.035 0.0225 0.0125" mass="0.06" rgba=".9 .3 .3 1" friction="0.6 0.005 0.0001"/></body>')
    arm = J_ROTOR * GEAR**2
    xml = f"""
<mujoco model="cdtp_twin">
  <option timestep="{PHYS_DT}" gravity="0 0 -9.81" integrator="implicitfast"/>
  <default><geom friction="1.0 0.01 0.001" condim="3"/></default>
  <worldbody>
    <geom name="floor" type="plane" size="5 5 0.1" rgba=".9 .9 .9 1"/>
    {post}
    <body name="chassis" pos="0 0 {R_WHEEL}">
      <freejoint name="root"/>
      {''.join(geoms)}
      {''.join(pocket)}
      {''.join(vee)}
      <site name="imu" pos="0 0 0.030"/>
      <site name="pin" pos="0.062 0 0.07"/>
      <body name="wheel_L" pos="0 {TRACK/2} 0">
        <joint name="wl" type="hinge" axis="0 1 0" armature="{arm}" damping="{B_VISC}" frictionloss="{TAU_C}"/>
        <geom type="cylinder" size="{R_WHEEL} 0.0125" euler="90 0 0" mass="0.07" rgba=".1 .1 .1 1"/>
      </body>
      <body name="wheel_R" pos="0 {-TRACK/2} 0">
        <joint name="wr" type="hinge" axis="0 1 0" armature="{arm}" damping="{B_VISC}" frictionloss="{TAU_C}"/>
        <geom type="cylinder" size="{R_WHEEL} 0.0125" euler="90 0 0" mass="0.07" rgba=".1 .1 .1 1"/>
      </body>
    </body>
    {box_body}
  </worldbody>
  <actuator>
    <motor name="ml" joint="wl" gear="1" ctrllimited="false"/>
    <motor name="mr" joint="wr" gear="1" ctrllimited="false"/>
  </actuator>
  <sensor>
    <accelerometer name="acc" site="imu"/>
    <gyro name="gyro" site="imu"/>
  </sensor>
</mujoco>"""
    return xml


class Twin:
    def __init__(self, x=0.0, y=0.0, yaw=0.0, post=None, task="statik", seed=1, box=True, bridge=True):
        self.m = mujoco.MjModel.from_xml_string(build_xml(post, box))
        self.d = mujoco.MjData(self.m)
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.post = post
        self.task = TASKS[task]
        q = self.d.qpos
        q[0:3] = [x, y, R_WHEEL]
        q[3:7] = [math.cos(yaw / 2), 0, 0, math.sin(yaw / 2)]
        if box:
            bj = self.m.joint(self.m.body("pillbox").jntadr[0])
            a = bj.qposadr[0]
            q[a:a + 3] = [x + KX * math.cos(yaw) - KY * math.sin(yaw), y + KX * math.sin(yaw) + KY * math.cos(yaw), q[a + 2]]
            q[a + 3:a + 7] = q[3:7]
            self.box_adr = a
        self.has_box = box
        self.jl, self.jr = self.m.joint("wl"), self.m.joint("wr")
        mujoco.mj_forward(self.m, self.d)
        self.t = 0.0
        self.duty = (0.0, 0.0)
        self.en = False
        self.mode, self.dock, self.theta_logic = 0, 0, 0.0
        self.filt = None
        self.ir = [0, 0]
        self.frame, self.burst = 0, 0
        self.glove = []        # [(t, cmd)]
        self.glove_next = 0.0
        self.motor_power = True
        self.hold = True       # elle dik tutuluyor
        self.magnet = True     # V-yuva miknatisi
        self.vbat = VOC
        self.trace = []
        self.proc = subprocess.Popen([str(BRIDGE)], stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
                                     bufsize=1) if bridge else None

    # ---- durum ----
    def rot(self):
        return self.d.xmat[self.m.body("chassis").id].reshape(3, 3)

    def pitch(self):
        R = self.rot()
        zb, xb = R[:, 2], R[:, 0]
        h = np.array([xb[0], xb[1], 0.0])
        h /= np.linalg.norm(h) + 1e-12
        return math.atan2(zb @ h, zb[2])

    def yaw(self):
        R = self.rot()
        return math.atan2(R[1, 0], R[0, 0])

    def pos(self):
        return self.d.qpos[0], self.d.qpos[1]

    def fwd_dist(self, x0, y0, yaw0):
        x, y = self.pos()
        return (x - x0) * math.cos(yaw0) + (y - y0) * math.sin(yaw0)

    def charging(self):
        if self.post is None:
            return False
        p = self.d.site("pin").xpos
        dist = math.hypot(p[0] - self.post[0], p[1] - self.post[1])
        return dist < 0.02 + 0.0015  # pim ucu seritten 3 mm disarida, yay yolu ~1,5 mm

    def box_ok(self):
        if not self.has_box:
            return True
        bp = self.d.qpos[self.box_adr:self.box_adr + 3]
        cid = self.m.body("chassis").id
        local = self.rot().T @ (bp - self.d.xpos[cid])
        return abs(local[0] - KX) < 0.03 and abs(local[1] - KY) < 0.03 and local[2] > 0.18

    # ---- sensorler ----
    def sensors(self):
        acc = self.d.sensor("acc").data.copy()
        gyro = self.d.sensor("gyro").data.copy()
        i_mot = abs(self.cur[0]) + abs(self.cur[1]) if hasattr(self, "cur") else 0
        vib = 0.3 * (0.3 + i_mot / 2) if self.en else 0
        acc += self.rng.normal(0, 0.03 + vib, 3)
        gyro += self.rng.normal(0, math.radians(0.05), 3)
        a = 1 - math.exp(-2 * math.pi * 42 / 200)  # MPU6050 DLPF
        raw = np.concatenate([acc, gyro])
        self.filt = raw if self.filt is None else self.filt + a * (raw - self.filt)
        return self.filt

    def enc(self):
        # gercek enkoder: teker ile govde (motor) arasindaki bagil aci
        mpc = 2 * math.pi / CPR
        cl = math.floor(self.d.qpos[self.jl.qposadr[0]] / mpc)
        cr = math.floor(self.d.qpos[self.jr.qposadr[0]] / mpc)
        return cl, -cr  # sag enkoder ayna takili

    def eye_gain(self, eye):
        if self.post is None:
            return 0.0
        R = self.rot()
        cid = self.m.body("chassis").id
        front = self.d.xpos[cid] + R @ np.array([0.06, 0, 0])
        dx, dy = self.post[0] - front[0], self.post[1] - front[1]
        dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
        if dist > 1.2 or dist < 0.01:
            return 0.0
        gamma = math.atan2(-dy, -dx)  # direk normaline (+X) gore robotun acisi
        emit = max(0.0, 1 - (gamma / math.radians(70)) ** 2)
        bearing = math.atan2(dy, dx) - self.yaw()
        bearing = (bearing + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi
        axis = math.radians(15 if eye == 0 else -15)
        acc = max(0.0, 1 - ((bearing - axis) / math.radians(30)) ** 2)
        g = emit * acc
        return 0.0 if g < 0.12 else min(1.0, 1.4 * g)

    def beacon(self):
        # 78 ms cerceve: ilk 45 ms'de 1,012 ms arayla 44 patlama, sonra 33 ms sessizlik (tamsayi sayacla)
        while True:
            tb = self.frame * 0.078 + self.burst * 0.0010122
            if tb > self.t:
                return
            for e in (0, 1):
                if self.rng.random() < self.eye_gain(e):
                    self.ir[e] += 1
            self.burst += 1
            if self.burst >= 44:
                self.burst = 0
                self.frame += 1

    def glove_cmd(self):
        c = "dur"
        for t, cmd in self.glove:
            if t <= self.t:
                c = cmd
        return CMD[c]

    # ---- adim ----
    def control(self):
        s = self.sensors()
        el, er = self.enc()
        gnew = 0
        if self.glove and self.t >= self.glove_next:
            self.glove_next = self.t + 0.05
            gnew = 1 if self.rng.random() > 0.05 else 0
        vchg = 12.4 if self.charging() else 0.0
        line = (f"{self.t:.4f} {s[0]:.5f} {s[1]:.5f} {s[2]:.5f} {s[3]:.6f} {s[4]:.6f} {s[5]:.6f} {el} {er} "
                f"{self.vbat:.3f} {vchg:.2f} {self.ir[0]} {self.ir[1]} {int(self.motor_power)} {gnew} {self.glove_cmd()} {self.task}\n")
        self.proc.stdin.write(line)
        out = self.proc.stdout.readline().split()
        self.en = out[0] == "1"
        dl, dr = float(out[1]), float(out[2])
        self.duty = (round(dl * 1023) / 1023, -round(dr * 1023) / 1023)  # sag motor ayna
        self.mode, self.dock, self.theta_logic = int(out[3]), int(out[4]), float(out[5])

    def physics(self):
        # DC motor: tork = Ke*(V - Ke*w)/R ; kuru/viskoz surtunme eklem parametrelerinde
        wl = self.d.qvel[self.jl.dofadr[0]]
        wr = self.d.qvel[self.jr.dofadr[0]]
        on = self.en and self.motor_power
        il = (self.duty[0] * self.vbat - KE * wl) / RA if on else 0.0
        ir = (self.duty[1] * self.vbat - KE * wr) / RA if on else 0.0
        self.cur = (il, ir)
        self.vbat = VOC - RINT * (abs(il) + abs(ir))
        self.d.ctrl[0] = KE * il
        self.d.ctrl[1] = KE * ir
        self.d.qfrc_applied[:] = 0
        if self.post is not None and self.magnet:
            p = self.d.site("pin").xpos
            dx, dy = self.post[0] - p[0], self.post[1] - p[1]
            dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
            gap = dist - 0.02
            if gap < MAGNET_RANGE and dist > 1e-6:
                f = MAGNET_N * (1 - max(gap, 0) / MAGNET_RANGE)
                force = np.array([dx / dist * f, dy / dist * f, 0.0])
                mujoco.mj_applyFT(self.m, self.d, force, np.zeros(3), p, self.m.body("chassis").id, self.d.qfrc_applied)
        if self.hold:  # elle dik tutma: govdeyi dik ve hareketsiz tut
            yaw = self.yaw()
            self.d.qpos[3:7] = [math.cos(yaw / 2), 0, 0, math.sin(yaw / 2)]
            self.d.qvel[0:6] = 0
        mujoco.mj_step(self.m, self.d)
        self.t += PHYS_DT
        self.beacon()

    def run(self, dur, each=None):
        n = int(round(dur / PHYS_DT))
        for i in range(n):
            if i % CTRL_EVERY == 0:
                self.control()
                if self.hold and self.mode == 1:  # DENGE'ye girdi: birak
                    self.hold = False
                x, y = self.pos()
                self.trace.append((self.t, x, y, math.degrees(self.pitch()), math.degrees(self.yaw()), self.duty[0],
                                   self.duty[1], self.mode, self.dock))
                if each:
                    each(self)
            self.physics()

    def arm(self):
        self.run(1.0)

    def tilt(self, deg):
        # govdeyi teker ekseni etrafinda egerek birak (elle egip birakma)
        R = self.rot()
        yb = R[:, 1]
        q = np.zeros(4)
        mujoco.mju_axisAngle2Quat(q, yb, math.radians(deg))
        q0 = self.d.qpos[3:7].copy()
        qn = np.zeros(4)
        mujoco.mju_mulQuat(qn, q, q0)
        cid = self.m.body("chassis").id
        axle = self.d.xpos[cid].copy()
        if self.has_box:  # kutu govdeyle birlikte egilir (elde tutulan robot)
            a = self.box_adr
            Rq = np.zeros(9)
            mujoco.mju_quat2Mat(Rq, q)
            rel = self.d.qpos[a:a + 3] - axle
            self.d.qpos[a:a + 3] = axle + Rq.reshape(3, 3) @ rel
            qb = np.zeros(4)
            mujoco.mju_mulQuat(qb, q, self.d.qpos[a + 3:a + 7].copy())
            self.d.qpos[a + 3:a + 7] = qb
            self.d.qvel[6:12] = 0
        self.d.qpos[3:7] = qn
        self.d.qpos[0:3] = axle
        self.d.qvel[0:6] = 0
        mujoco.mj_forward(self.m, self.d)

    def lying(self):
        return abs(self.pitch()) > math.radians(40)  # govde ~45-50 derecede plakayla yere degiyor

    def close(self):
        if self.proc:
            self.proc.stdin.write("quit\n")
            self.proc.stdin.close()
            self.proc.wait()

    def csv(self, name):
        with open(OUT / name, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("t,x,y,theta_deg,yaw_deg,dutyL,dutyR,mode,dock\n")
            for r in self.trace:
                f.write(",".join(f"{v:.5f}" if isinstance(v, float) else str(v) for v in r) + "\n")


# ======================= senaryolar =======================
RESULTS = []


def report(tid, name, ok, detail):
    RESULTS.append((tid, name, "GECTI" if ok else "KALDI", detail))
    print(f"[{'GECTI' if ok else 'KALDI'}] {tid:7s} {name:44s} {detail}", flush=True)


class Watch:
    def __init__(self):
        self.max_th, self.fell, self.box = 0.0, False, True

    def __call__(self, tw):
        if not tw.hold:
            self.max_th = max(self.max_th, abs(tw.pitch()))
        self.fell |= tw.lying() or tw.mode == 4
        self.box &= tw.box_ok()


def t_passive():
    worst = 0.0
    ok = True
    for deg in (10, -10):
        tw = Twin(bridge=False)
        tw.hold = False
        tw.tilt(deg)
        t = 0.0
        while t < 5 and not tw.lying():
            mujoco.mj_step(tw.m, tw.d)
            t += PHYS_DT
        ok &= t < 5
        worst = max(worst, t)
    report("ON", "Enerjisiz +-10 deg'den dik kalmamali", ok, f"40 deg'ye en gec {worst:.2f} s'de dustu")


def t_static():
    tw = Twin(task="statik", seed=1)
    tw.arm()
    w = Watch()
    x0, y0 = tw.pos()
    maxd = [0.0]

    def each(t):
        w(t)
        maxd[0] = max(maxd[0], math.hypot(t.pos()[0] - x0, t.pos()[1] - y0))
    tw.run(30, each)
    tw.csv("ikiz_statik.csv")
    tw.close()
    ok = not w.fell and w.box and w.max_th < math.radians(5) and maxd[0] < 0.15
    report("C1.1", "30 s statik denge (kutulu)", ok,
           f"en buyuk egim {math.degrees(w.max_th):.2f} deg, kayma {maxd[0]*100:.1f} cm, kutu {'yerinde' if w.box else 'DUSTU'}")


def t_move(forward=True):
    c = "ileri" if forward else "geri"
    tw = Twin(task="yonlendirme", seed=2 if forward else 3)
    tw.glove = [(0, "dur"), (6.0, c), (6.4, "dur"), (9.5, c), (9.9, "dur")]
    tw.arm()
    w = Watch()
    tw.run(4.9, w)  # 5 s dengede bekleme
    yaw0 = tw.yaw()
    x0, y0 = tw.pos()
    tw.run(8.1, w)  # C++ testiyle ayni: durma penceresi t=14 s'de baslar
    d0 = tw.fwd_dist(x0, y0, yaw0)
    maxdx = [0.0]

    def each(t):
        w(t)
        maxdx[0] = max(maxdx[0], abs(t.fwd_dist(x0, y0, yaw0) - d0))
    tw.run(3.0, each)
    d = abs(tw.fwd_dist(x0, y0, yaw0))
    tw.csv("ikiz_ileri.csv" if forward else "ikiz_geri.csv")
    tw.close()
    ok = not w.fell and w.box and 0.9 < d < 1.1 and maxdx[0] < 0.03
    report("C1.2" if forward else "C1.3", f"1 m {c} + 3 s dur", ok,
           f"yol {d:.3f} m, 3 s durmada kayma {maxdx[0]*1000:.1f} mm, egim tepe {math.degrees(w.max_th):.1f} deg, kutu {'yerinde' if w.box else 'DUSTU'}")


def t_recovery():
    res = []
    for deg in (10, -10):
        tw = Twin(task="statik", seed=4)
        tw.arm()
        tw.tilt(deg)
        t0 = tw.t
        rec = [None]
        after = [0.0]
        w = Watch()

        # toparlanma: |theta| < 3 deg ve |v| < 0,1 m/s'ye ilk varis; sonrasinda egim 5 deg'yi hic asmamali (C++ testiyle ayni olcut)
        def each(t):
            w(t)
            th = abs(t.pitch())
            v = np.linalg.norm(t.d.qvel[0:2])
            if rec[0] is None and th < math.radians(3) and v < 0.1:
                rec[0] = t.t - t0
            if rec[0] is not None:
                after[0] = max(after[0], th)
        tw.run(10, each)
        if after[0] > math.radians(5):
            rec[0] = None
        tw.close()
        res.append((deg, rec[0], w.fell, w.box))
    ok = all(r[1] is not None and r[1] < 10 and not r[2] and r[3] for r in res)
    report("C1.4", "+10 ve -10 deg'den 10 s icinde toparlanma", ok,
           ", ".join(f"{r[0]:+d}: {r[1]:.2f} s" if r[1] is not None else f"{r[0]:+d}: TOPARLANMADI" for r in res))


def t_turn():
    out = []
    for c, sgn in (("sol", 1), ("sag", -1)):
        tw = Twin(task="yonlendirme", seed=5)
        tw.glove = [(0, "dur"), (1.5, c), (1.9, "dur")]
        tw.arm()
        y0 = tw.yaw()
        w = Watch()
        tw.run(5, w)
        dy = math.degrees((tw.yaw() - y0 + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi)
        tw.close()
        out.append((c, dy, not w.fell and w.box and abs(dy - 90 * sgn) < 15))
    report("C2.3-4", "Eldiven: sola / saga ~90 deg", all(o[2] for o in out),
           ", ".join(f"{o[0]} {o[1]:+.1f} deg" for o in out))


def t_estop():
    tw = Twin(task="statik", seed=6)
    tw.arm()
    tw.motor_power = False
    tw.run(0.05)
    ok = tw.mode == 0 and not tw.en
    tw.close()
    report("GUV.2", "Acil stop: denge birakilir, motorlar kapanir", ok, f"mod={['BEKLE','DENGE','YANASMA','SARJ','DUSTU'][tw.mode]}")


def dock_once(rho, alpha_deg, head_deg, seed, record=False):
    # direk dunya orijininde; yaklasma bolgesi direk normali (+X) etrafinda
    a = math.radians(alpha_deg)
    x, y = rho * math.cos(a), rho * math.sin(a)
    yaw = math.atan2(-y, -x) + math.radians(head_deg)
    tw = Twin(x=x, y=y, yaw=yaw, post=(0.0, 0.0), task="sarj", seed=seed)
    tw.arm()
    t0 = tw.t
    w = Watch()
    st = {"contact": None, "charge": None}

    def each(t):
        w(t)
        if st["contact"] is None and t.charging():
            st["contact"] = t.t
        if st["charge"] is None and t.mode == 3:
            st["charge"] = t.t
    tw.run(45, each)
    if record:
        tw.csv("ikiz_yanasma.csv")
    tw.close()
    ok = st["charge"] is not None and tw.charging() and not w.box is False
    return ok, (st["contact"] - t0) if st["contact"] else None, (st["charge"] - st["contact"]) if st["charge"] and st["contact"] else None


def t_dock():
    cases = [(r, a, h) for r in (0.18, 0.30) for a in (-45, -20, 0, 20, 45) for h in (-25, 0, 25)]
    good, worst, wc = 0, 0.0, 0.0
    fails = []
    for i, (r, a, h) in enumerate(cases):
        ok, tc, tchg = dock_once(r, a, h, 100 + i, record=(i == len(cases) - 2))
        if ok:
            good += 1
            worst = max(worst, tc)
            wc = max(wc, tchg)
        else:
            fails.append(f"({r:.2f} m, {a} deg, bakis {h:+d})")
    report("C3", f"IR ile yanasma, V-yuva + direk ({len(cases)} baslangic)", good == len(cases),
           f"{good}/{len(cases)} basarili, en uzun temas {worst:.1f} s, temas->sarj modu {wc:.2f} s " + " ".join(fails))


def t_undock():
    """Sarjdayken eldivenle 'geri': robot miknatistan kopup dengeye girer ve 0,5 m geri gider."""
    tw = Twin(x=0.25, y=0.0, yaw=math.pi, post=(0.0, 0.0), task="sarj", seed=8)
    tw.arm()
    tw.run(15)
    docked = tw.mode == 3 and tw.charging()
    tw.glove = [(0, "dur"), (tw.t + 0.5, "geri"), (tw.t + 0.9, "dur")]
    w = Watch()
    tw.run(8, w)
    p = tw.d.site("pin").xpos
    gap = math.hypot(p[0], p[1]) - 0.02
    ok = docked and tw.mode == 1 and gap > 0.25 and not w.fell
    tw.close()
    report("C3.5", "Eldivenle 'geri': miknatistan kopup istasyondan ayrilir", ok,
           f"once {'SARJ' if docked else 'sarjda degil'}, sonra mod {['BEKLE','DENGE','YANASMA','SARJ','DUSTU'][tw.mode]}, direge uzaklik {gap*100:.0f} cm")


def t_integrated():
    # direk (0,0); robot (0.27,-1.0)'dan +Y'ye bakarak baslar: 2 x ileri, 5 s bekle, sola don, otonom yanasma
    tw = Twin(x=0.27, y=-1.0, yaw=math.pi / 2, post=(0.0, 0.0), task="butunlesik", seed=7)
    tw.glove = [(0, "dur"), (5.0, "ileri"), (5.4, "dur"), (8.6, "ileri"), (9.0, "dur"), (17.0, "sol"), (17.4, "dur")]
    tw.arm()
    w = Watch()
    early = [False]

    def each(t):
        w(t)
        if t.t < 17 and t.mode == 2:
            early[0] = True
    tw.run(60, each)
    tw.csv("ikiz_butunlesik.csv")
    ok = tw.mode == 3 and tw.charging() and not early[0] and w.box
    tw.close()
    report("C5", "Butunlesik gorev (kutu, eldiven, yanasma)", ok,
           f"son mod {['BEKLE','DENGE','YANASMA','SARJ','DUSTU'][tw.mode]}, erken yanasma {'VAR' if early[0] else 'yok'}, kutu {'yerinde' if w.box else 'DUSTU'}")


def t_reference_gains():
    """GitHub: suelioalencar/Two-Wheels-Inverted-Pendulum... gercek robotta calisan LQI kazanclari
    (k1 = [-100.5, -69.9, -83.6, -9.31] V, 65 mm teker, enkoderli DC motor). Bu kazanclari ikizde, kendi
    durum olcumleriyle (dogrudan fizikten) kosup modelin gercekci bir bolgede oldugunu kontrol eder."""
    K = np.array([-100.493, -69.900, -83.636, -9.314])
    res = []
    for deg in (0, 8):
        tw = Twin(task="statik", seed=9, bridge=False)
        tw.hold = False
        tw.en = True
        if deg:
            tw.tilt(deg)
        x0 = tw.d.qpos[0]
        ok = True
        prev = tw.pitch()
        for i in range(int(8 / PHYS_DT)):
            if i % CTRL_EVERY == 0:
                th = tw.pitch()
                om = tw.d.sensor("gyro").data[1]
                wl = tw.d.qpos[tw.jl.qposadr[0]]
                wr = tw.d.qpos[tw.jr.qposadr[0]]
                x = R_WHEEL * ((wl + wr) / 2 + th) - 0  # teker yolu
                v = R_WHEEL * ((tw.d.qvel[tw.jl.dofadr[0]] + tw.d.qvel[tw.jr.dofadr[0]]) / 2 + om)
                u = -(K @ np.array([x, v, th, om]))
                u = max(-11.0, min(11.0, u))
                tw.duty = (u / tw.vbat, u / tw.vbat)
            tw.physics()
            if tw.lying():
                ok = False
                break
        res.append((deg, ok, math.degrees(tw.pitch())))
    report("REF", "GitHub referans LQI kazanclari ikizde dengeliyor", all(r[1] for r in res),
           ", ".join(f"baslangic {r[0]} deg: {'dengede' if r[1] else 'DUSTU'} (son {r[2]:+.1f} deg)" for r in res))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    which = sys.argv[1:] or ["passive", "static", "move", "recovery", "turn", "estop", "ref", "dock", "undock", "integrated"]
    if "passive" in which: t_passive()
    if "static" in which: t_static()
    if "move" in which: t_move(True); t_move(False)
    if "recovery" in which: t_recovery()
    if "turn" in which: t_turn()
    if "estop" in which: t_estop()
    if "ref" in which: t_reference_gains()
    if "dock" in which: t_dock()
    if "undock" in which: t_undock()
    if "integrated" in which: t_integrated()
    with open(OUT / "summary.tsv", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for r in RESULTS:
            f.write("\t".join(r) + "\n")
    n = sum(r[2] == "GECTI" for r in RESULTS)
    print(f"\nIKIZ OZET: {n}/{len(RESULTS)} gecti")
twin/bridge.cpp 52 satır
// Dijital ikiz koprusu: robot firmware'indeki RobotLogic'i stdin/stdout uzerinden MuJoCo ikizine baglar.
// Her satir bir 5 ms adimi:
//   girdi : t ax ay az gx gy gz encL encR vbat vchg irL irR motorPower gloveNew gloveCmd task
//   cikti : en dutyL dutyR mode dock thetaDeg
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

#include "robot_logic.h"

int main() {
  cdtp::RobotLogic logic;
  char line[512];
  uint16_t seq = 0;
  int lastTask = -1;
  while (std::fgets(line, sizeof line, stdin)) {
    if (!std::strncmp(line, "quit", 4)) break;
    cdtp::RobotInputs in;
    int en_mp, gnew, gcmd, task;
    long eL, eR, iL, iR;
    int n = std::sscanf(line, "%f %f %f %f %f %f %f %ld %ld %f %f %ld %ld %d %d %d %d", &in.t, &in.imu.acc[0],
                        &in.imu.acc[1], &in.imu.acc[2], &in.imu.gyro[0], &in.imu.gyro[1], &in.imu.gyro[2], &eL, &eR,
                        &in.vbat, &in.vcharge, &iL, &iR, &en_mp, &gnew, &gcmd, &task);
    if (n != 17) {
      std::printf("ERR %d\n", n);
      std::fflush(stdout);
      continue;
    }
    if (task != lastTask) {
      logic.setTask((cdtp::Task)task);
      lastTask = task;
    }
    in.encL = eL;
    in.encR = eR;
    in.irL = iL;
    in.irR = iR;
    in.motorPower = en_mp != 0;
    in.gloveNew = gnew != 0;
    if (in.gloveNew) {
      cdtp::GlovePacket p{};
      p.cmd = (uint8_t)gcmd;
      p.seq = seq++;
      cdtp::sealPacket(p);
      in.glove = p;
    }
    cdtp::RobotOutputs o = logic.step(in);
    std::printf("%d %.5f %.5f %d %d %.3f\n", o.motorEnable ? 1 : 0, o.dutyL, o.dutyR, (int)logic.mode(),
                (int)logic.dockState(), logic.thetaDeg());
    std::fflush(stdout);
  }
  return 0;
}
tools/plot_twin.py 74 satır
"""C++ simulasyonu ile MuJoCo dijital ikizini karsilastiran grafikler -> docs/img/ikiz_*.png"""
import csv
import math
import pathlib

import matplotlib

matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
OUT = ROOT / "docs/img"


def load(p):
    with open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    return {k: [float(r[k]) for r in rows] for k in rows[0]}


def style(ax):
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    ax.spines[["top", "right"]].set_visible(False)


def compare():
    c = load(ROOT / "sim/results/c1_ileri.csv")
    t = load(ROOT / "twin/results/ikiz_ileri.csv")
    tx0, ty0 = t["x"][0], t["y"][0]
    td = [math.hypot(x - tx0, y - ty0) for x, y in zip(t["x"], t["y"])]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(8, 5.4), sharex=True)
    ax[0].plot(c["t"], c["x"], color="#2a6fdb", label="C++ simülasyonu")
    ax[0].plot(t["t"], td, color="#d9822b", label="MuJoCo dijital ikiz", lw=1.2)
    ax[0].set_ylabel("yol (m)")
    ax[0].legend(frameon=False, loc="upper left")
    ax[1].plot(c["t"], c["theta_deg"], color="#2a6fdb")
    ax[1].plot(t["t"], t["theta_deg"], color="#d9822b", lw=1.2)
    ax[1].set_ylabel("eğim (°)")
    ax[1].set_xlabel("zaman (s)")
    for a in ax:
        style(a)
    fig.suptitle("Aynı firmware mantığı, iki bağımsız model: 5 s bekle, 2 × ileri, 3 s dur")
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(OUT / "ikiz_karsilastirma.png", dpi=110)
    plt.close(fig)


def dock():
    p = ROOT / "twin/results/ikiz_yanasma.csv"
    if not p.exists():
        return
    d = load(p)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 4.6))
    ax.plot(d["x"], d["y"], color="#d9822b", label="robot merkezi (ikiz)")
    ax.plot(d["x"][0], d["y"][0], "o", color="#d9822b")
    ax.add_patch(plt.Circle((0, 0), 0.02, color="#333", label="direk Ø40"))
    th = [math.radians(a) for a in range(-45, 46)]
    ax.plot([0.3 * math.cos(a) for a in th], [0.3 * math.sin(a) for a in th], "--", color="#999", lw=0.8, label="30 cm / 90° bölge")
    ax.set_aspect("equal")
    ax.set_xlabel("X (m)")
    ax.set_ylabel("Y (m)")
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=8)
    style(ax)
    ax.set_title("Dijital ikiz: IR ile yanaşma")
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(OUT / "ikiz_yanasma.png", dpi=110)
    plt.close(fig)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    compare()
    dock()
    print("cizildi")

"""3B baski parcalarinin tum olculeri (mm). Eksenler: X ileri, Y sol, Z yukari; orijin teker ekseninin ortasi.
Zemin z = -35. Parca gelince kumpasla olcun; (OLC) isaretlileri saticiya gore degisir.
Degistirdikten sonra: python cad/robot_cad.py  (STL'ler ve kontrol raporu yeniden uretilir)."""

# --- Genel ---
